# ERRORS.md — the ERROR-SURFACE TABLE (Phase A / gate for Phase C)

Mechanically derived from the C source in `c_src/libsodium`: **every** distinct
way the library rejects or errors on input — every `return -1` / `return NULL` /
error-enum return, every `sodium_misuse()` (which runs the installed handler and
then `abort()`s → SIGABRT), every active `assert()`, every explicit range / bounds
/ overflow check, every NULL check the code itself performs, every `errno = ...`
assignment, and every out-of-range enum / `alg` / variant value.

Build configuration these rows describe (see `c_src/CMakeLists.txt`): **no `HAVE_*`
macros** and **no `NDEBUG`** — so every `#ifdef HAVE_*` selects the portable
fallback and `assert()` is live. Target: x86-64 Linux, `SIZE_MAX == 2^64-1`.

Column 4 is the `file:line` the row was derived from. The `test` column names the
differential test that constructs the condition and asserts C and Rust reject it
identically (same error code / sentinel / signal / side effects), or states why the
row is not constructible.

## Row counts

| group | scope | rows |
|---|---|---|
| **G1** | `sodium/` + `randombytes/` — `sodium_*` utils, codecs, core, runtime, version, RNG | 159 |
| **G2** | `crypto_pwhash/` — argon2i / argon2id / scrypt | 223 |
| **G3** | `crypto_aead/` — aegis128l, aegis256, aes256gcm, chacha20poly1305, xchacha20poly1305 | 80 |
| **G4** | hashes / MACs / XOFs / KDFs — blake2b, sha2, sha3, shake, turboshake, keccak1600, poly1305, siphash, verify, hkdf, kdf_blake2b | 135 |
| **G5** | `crypto_box` / `crypto_secretbox` / `crypto_secretstream` / `crypto_kx` / `crypto_sign` / `crypto_auth` | 110 |
| **G6** | `crypto_core` / `crypto_scalarmult` / `crypto_stream` / `crypto_kem` / `crypto_ipcrypt` | 149 |
| | **TOTAL** | **856** |

## G1 — `sodium/` + `randombytes/` — `sodium_*` utils, codecs, core, runtime, version, RNG

| # | function | trigger (the exact invalid input/condition) | expected C result | source | test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| G1-001 | `sodium_bin2hex` | `bin_len >= SIZE_MAX / 2`, e.g. `bin_len = 0x7FFFFFFFFFFFFFFF`, any `hex`/`hex_maxlen` (bin never dereferenced before the check) | `SIGABRT` (sodium_misuse -> abort) | codecs.c:23 | `t10_sodium::*`, `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::*`, `t11_sodium_errors::no_rejection_path_rows` |
| G1-002 | `sodium_bin2hex` | `bin_len = SIZE_MAX` (upper clause of same test) | `SIGABRT` (sodium_misuse) | codecs.c:23 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::no_rejection_path_rows` |
| G1-003 | `sodium_bin2hex` | `hex_maxlen <= bin_len * 2U` with no room for the NUL, e.g. `bin_len=4, hex_maxlen=8` | `SIGABRT` (sodium_misuse) | codecs.c:23 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::no_rejection_path_rows` |
| G1-004 | `sodium_bin2hex` | `bin_len=0, hex_maxlen=0` (0 <= 0 is true, so even the empty case needs 1 byte) | `SIGABRT` (sodium_misuse) | codecs.c:23 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::no_rejection_path_rows` |
| G1-005 | `sodium_bin2hex` | `bin_len=1, hex_maxlen=2` | `SIGABRT` (sodium_misuse) | codecs.c:23 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::no_rejection_path_rows` |
| G1-006 | `sodium_hex2bin` | output buffer full: `bin_maxlen=1, hex="0011", hex_len=4, ignore=NULL, hex_end!=NULL` | returns `-1`, `errno=ERANGE`, `*bin_len=0`, `*hex_end=&hex[2]` | codecs.c:71-74 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::hex2bin_rejections` |
| G1-007 | `sodium_hex2bin` | `bin_maxlen=0, hex="00", hex_len=2, hex_end!=NULL` (fails on the very first nibble pair) | returns `-1`, `errno=ERANGE`, `*bin_len=0`, `*hex_end=&hex[0]` | codecs.c:71-74 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t10_sodium::hex2bin_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::hex2bin_rejections` |
| G1-008 | `sodium_hex2bin` | same ERANGE case but `hex_end=NULL`: `bin_maxlen=1, hex="0011", hex_len=4` | returns `-1`, but `errno` is overwritten to `EINVAL` by the hex_end==NULL branch, `*bin_len=0` | codecs.c:71-74 then 94-96 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t10_sodium::hex2bin_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::hex2bin_rejections` |
| G1-009 | `sodium_hex2bin` | odd number of hex digits: `hex="abc", hex_len=3, bin_maxlen=2, hex_end!=NULL` | returns `-1`, `errno=EINVAL`, `*bin_len=0`, `hex_pos` decremented so `*hex_end=&hex[2]` | codecs.c:84-88 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t10_sodium::hex2bin_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::hex2bin_rejections` |
| G1-010 | `sodium_hex2bin` | single hex digit: `hex="a", hex_len=1, bin_maxlen=1` | returns `-1`, `errno=EINVAL`, `*bin_len=0`, `*hex_end=&hex[0]` | codecs.c:84-88 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t10_sodium::hex2bin_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::hex2bin_rejections` |
| G1-011 | `sodium_hex2bin` | ignore char lands mid-byte (state != 0): `ignore=":", hex="0:0", hex_len=3` (the `state==0U` guard blocks the skip) | returns `-1`, `errno=EINVAL`, `*bin_len=0`, `*hex_end=&hex[0]` | codecs.c:64 then 84-88 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t10_sodium::hex2bin_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::hex2bin_rejections` |
| G1-012 | `sodium_hex2bin` | trailing garbage with `hex_end=NULL`: `hex="00zz", hex_len=4, bin_maxlen=4, ignore=NULL` | returns `-1`, `errno=EINVAL`, but `*bin_len=1` and `bin[0]=0x00` (bin_pos is zeroed at :90 BEFORE this branch, so it is NOT reset) | codecs.c:94-96 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t10_sodium::hex2bin_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::hex2bin_rejections` |
| G1-013 | `sodium_hex2bin` | non-hex, non-ignored char with `hex_end=NULL`: `ignore=":", hex="00-11", hex_len=5` | returns `-1`, `errno=EINVAL`, `*bin_len=1` (not reset) | codecs.c:94-96 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t10_sodium::hex2bin_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::hex2bin_rejections` |
| G1-014 | `sodium_hex2bin` | `hex_len=1, hex=":"`, `ignore=":"`, `hex_end=NULL`: ignored char consumed, hex_pos==hex_len | returns `0`, `*bin_len=0` (NOT an error) | codecs.c:64-67, 94 | `t10_sodium::bin2hex_valid`, `t10_sodium::hex2bin_valid`, `t11_sodium_errors::hex2bin_rejections` |
